CastHoursForFile|words|0|12|30|Opening~hours,~as~everybody~on~the~street~knows~them.~The~corner~shop:~nine~till~half~five~every~day;~open~now.~The~all-night~garage:~day~and~night~every~day;~open~now.~The~market:~Tuesdays,~Fridays~and~Saturdays,~eight~till~four;~shut~now.~Threerun:~Monday~to~Wednesday,~half~six~in~the~morning~till~one;~open~now.~The~chemist:~Mondays,~nine~till~twelve,~Thursdays,~two~till~eight~at~night;~shut~now.~The~baker's:~nine~till~half~five,~on~Saturdays~it~opens~at~ten,~on~Sundays~it~shuts~at~one;~open~now.~The~dairy:~seven~till~seven,~on~Fridays~eight~till~eleven~at~night,~shut~on~Saturdays~and~Sundays;~open~now.~The~tailor's:~nine~till~five,~shut~on~Friday~to~Sunday,~by~appointment~on~Fridays;~open~now.~The~ironmonger's:~nine~till~five,~on~Thursday~to~Saturday~ten~till~six,~shut~on~Sundays;~open~now.~The~post~office:~nine~till~six,~on~Mondays~shut~from~twelve~till~one~and~from~three~till~half~three,~on~Tuesdays~and~Wednesdays~shut~from~twelve~till~one,~on~Thursdays~shut~from~twelve~till~one~and~from~one~till~two,~on~Fridays~shut~from~half~twelve~till~half~one,~on~Saturdays~shut~from~eleven~till~twelve~and~from~three~till~four,~shut~on~Sundays;~shut~now.~The~snooker~hall:~six~in~the~evening~till~two~in~the~morning,~on~Sundays~seven~in~the~evening~till~six~in~the~morning,~on~Saturdays~shut~from~half~twelve~in~the~morning~till~one~in~the~evening;~shut~now.~The~coffee~stall:~midnight~till~seven~in~the~morning,~on~Tuesdays~half~twelve~in~the~morning~till~twelve,~on~Wednesdays~seven~till~midnight,~on~Thursdays~half~six~in~the~morning~till~half~twelve~in~the~morning,~on~Fridays~it~opens~at~half~twelve~in~the~morning,~shut~on~Saturdays~and~Sundays;~shut~now.~The~shop:~Mondays,~nine~till~five;~shut~now.
